        /// <summary>
        /// Status of bit field when parsing. Options are: 0=IsAccepted, 1=IsFilled, 2=IsUpdated, 3=IsFinished. This is mostly for internal use
        /// </summary>
        public long? Status { get; init; } //Status

        /// <summary>
